# FAN LIFE — All Royal Rumble Players: Position and Pricing Audit

**Source:** `maordubel/fanlife` main, snapshot 2026-10-11. **Pricing version:** `rumble-economy-v2`. This file is a **full price-ID census**, not an independently verified historical revaluation. Prices are **game currency (€M)**, not real-world valuations.

## Editorial rules
- Each row has a stable club/player ID and the exact production V3 game price; no record has been repriced by guesswork.
- NAME TO VERIFY indicates the ID was not resolved in the indexed master and 10-Oct roster-wave files; Claude must reconcile the remaining club source packs before applying data changes.
- UNVERIFIED means no defensible position in these sources. Do not fill it by random assignment.
- Keep €5M owner-approved icons pinned. Review any suggested adjustment through a separate diff and V4 validation.
- Prioritize review of €1M players with substantial historical records; quota distribution alone must not override genuine footballing strength.

## Claude integration checklist
1. Load this audit and the canonical files; join by `(clubId,playerId)` and never name only.
2. Complete identity gaps against core, research, legacy waves and review manifests. Compare all in-game candidate counts.
3. Fill only source-proven primary/secondary positions, with provenance, and leave remaining unknown in a review queue.
4. Independently review rating/price by sporting quality, era, competitive legacy and positional balance. Offer an old/new price diff and justification for owner approval.
5. Validate economic tier quotas, pinned icons, role distribution, lineup eligibility, duplicate IDs, name aliases, and all game pools before applying.

## Primary references
- `content/generated/rumble-prices-v3.json` (actual in-game prices)
- `content/generated/player-master.json` (Hapoel master)
- `content/manual/player-positions-extra.json` (Wikipedia infobox supplement)
- `club-packs/*/wave-roster-2026-10-10.json` (October intake)
- `docs/fanlife/49-rumble-economy-v2-review.md` (rules)

**Caution:** Unverified is not a claim of absence from all club archives. This audit is complete for the 5,608 V3 IDs and their actual prices; position/name enrichment remains incomplete until every historic club-pack source is reconciled.

### Remaining source reconciliation
The historical `core.json` and `wave-auto.json` packages require a subsequent identity-aware join. No name or role was inferred from them in this export. The summary and row flags are based on the master and 2026-10-10 roster wave only; unresolved records must be researched before being treated as complete.
